// Main Quest
prontera,121,72,3	script	Royal Messenger#e16_pr	4_M_ZONDAOYAJI,5,5,{
	if (checkweight(1301,5) == 0) {
		mes "- You nao pode proceed com o missao desde you have tambem muitos itens. -";
		close;
	}
	switch( ep16_royal ) {
	case 0:
		if (BaseLevel < 100 || (ep13_ryu < 100 && ep13_start < 100)) {// Access to the New World pre-req (Onward to the New World Quest or Cat Paw Agent Quest)
			mes "[Royal Messenger]";
			mes "Ola!";
			mes "Agorum ^4d4dffo palace is hosting um banquet fou o returning heroes de Vacuum^000000.";
			mes "Pou favou me digum ^4d4dffwhere o heroes who have participated in o expedition are^000000 se voce sabe any.";
			next;
			mes "[Royal Messenger]";
			mes "I imaginar where they are. Eles sao entao scattered esse it is dificil to locate each one of them. Hahaha!";
			close;
		}
		mes "[Royal Messenger]";
		mes "Oh, esperar. You are...";
		mes "" + strcharinfo(0) + ", certo?";
		mes "Vamos ver.";
		next;
		mes "[Royal Messenger]";
		mes "Eu sou certo. Voce parece exatamente o mesmo as o montage de o guilda. Hahahaha!";
		mes "Meu olhos are fazendo what they are supposed to do agora, I guess.";
		next;
		mes "[Royal Messenger]";
		mes "Isentao e dificil to encontrar o freelance aventureiros who nao pertencer to o military.";
		mes "Erum muito dificil to encontrar you desde you are nao bound to qualquer lugar.";
		next;
		select("May I ajudar you?");
		mes "[Royal Messenger]";
		mes "Ajudar? Sure coisa!";
		mes "Voce sabe esse hum um banquet levando lugar in celebration of subjugating Morocc.";
		mes "Um invitation tem sido issued fou you. Mas erum apenas impossivel to meet you.";
		next;
		mes "[Royal Messenger]";
		mes "Eu sou sure esse todos o Royal Messengers of o cidade deve ser procurando fou com o portrait of you.";
		mes "Hey, desde we nao have suficiente tempo, pou favou pegar este invitation fou you.";
		next;
		mes "[Royal Messenger]";
		mes "Voce deve attend o banquet. Voce entender? ^4d4dffVoce tem been invited as um especial guest^000000.";
		next;
		unittalk getcharid(3), "" + strcharinfo(0) + " : A special guest?", bc_self;
		mes "[Royal Messenger]";
		mes "O pessoas in o palace will fill you up com o detalhes.";
		mes "Eu sou apenas um messenger depois todos.";
		mes "Emborum o banquet sera held fou quite um while, nao pegar tambem muito tempo to pegar la.";
		next;
		mes "[Royal Messenger]";
		mes "Fazer sure to trazer o invitation com you. Nao esquecer!";
		mes "Why voce nao ir agorum mesmo? ^4d4dffEu consigo teleport you lum agorum se you querer^000000.";
		getitem 6933,1;// Banquet_Invitation
		setquest 7681;// Invitation to Royal Banquet
		ep16_royal = 1;
		next;
		if (select( "Eu vou ir on meu own.", "Eu vou ir certo embora." ) == 1) {
			mes "[Royal Messenger]";
			mes "Voce pode visit o Pronterum Palace.";
			mes "Voce pode show o invitation to o guardum at o gate or";
			mes "A servant in charge of o banquet.";
			next;
			mes "[Royal Messenger]";
			mes "Since Eu tenho delivered o invitation to you, I shall esperar fou outro one.";
			mes "I esperancum you have um bom tempo la, dear heroi.";
			close;
		}
		mes "[Royal Messenger]";
		mes "Deixe-me teleport you lum certo embora.";
		mes "When you arrive, lum sera um servant fou reception. Voce pode show o invitation entao.";
		close2;
		warp "prontera",156,327;
		end;
	case 1:
		mes "[Royal Messenger]";
		mes "Pou que you aindum aqui?";
		mes "What cum I do fou you?";
		next;
		if (select( "Send me to o Banquet Hall.", "Eu tenho lost o invitation." ) == 2) {
			if (countitem(6933) < 1) {
				mes "[Royal Messenger]";
				mes "What um shame! O que aconteceu?";
				mes "Bem, desde o message esse voce vai be lum has jum been sent la,";
				mes "Voce pode nao realmente precisar o invitation anyways.";
				next;
				mes "[Royal Messenger]";
				mes "When you ir to o palace, o servant in charge will recognize you.";
				mes "Nao preocupar, e voce pode ir to o palace agora.";
				close;
			}
			mes "[Royal Messenger]";
			mes "Eh? O que e esse you have in seu pocket? It looks like um invitation to me.";
			mes "I may have um tiny definir of olhos, mas I nao irium miss it.";
			next;
			mes "[Royal Messenger]";
			mes "Since you have encontrou seu invitation, vamos pegar indo! Hahahaha!";
			close;
		}
		mes "[Royal Messenger]";
		mes "Deixe-me teleport you lum certo embora.";
		mes "When you arrive, lum sera um servant fou reception. Voce pode show o invitation entao.";
		close2;
		warp "prontera",156,327;
		end;
	default:
		mes "[Royal Messenger]";
		mes "Eu tenho ouviu esse you have attended o banquet safely.";
		mes "Eu precientao de to encontrar o others e send them to o banquet as bem.";
		close2;
		npctalk "I want to go home...", "", bc_self;
		end;
	}

OnTouch:
	if (ep16_royal == 0 && BaseLevel > 99 && (ep13_ryu > 99 || ep13_start > 99))
		npctalk "Wait! You must be... " + strcharinfo(0) + ". I have something to tell you!", "", bc_self;
	else
		npctalk "I am looking for the hero who returned from the expedition...", "", bc_self;
	end;
}

prontera,150,331,4	script	Royal Guard#ep16_ect01	8W_SOLDIER,4,4,{
	if (ep16_royal == 1) {
		mes "[Guard]";
		mes "Aqui comes o heroi!";
		mes "O Banquet Hall is dentro o palace. Voce pode seguir o passage to pegar la.";
		mes "Deixe-me send um message fou o Chamberlain to encontrar you.";
		close;
	}
	npctalk "Welcome to the glorious Prontera Palace in the heart of Rune-Midgarts!", "Royal Guard#ep16_ect01", bc_self;// Royal Guard#ep16_ect01 only
	end;

OnTouch:
	if (ep16_royal > 0) {
		npctalk "Eternal glory to the hero of the Midgards!", "Royal Guard#ep16_ect01", bc_self;
		npctalk "Eternal glory to the hero of the Midgards!", "Royal Guard#ep16_ect02", bc_self;
	}
	end;
}

prt_cas,215,146,0	script	#ep16_evt_01_on	HIDDEN_WARP_NPC,1,1,{
	end;
OnTouch:
	if (isbegin_quest(7681) == 1) {
		cloakoffnpcself( "Chamberlain Bell#ep16_01" );
		setpcblock PCBLOCK_NPC, true;
		sleep2 3000;
		setpcblock PCBLOCK_NPC, false;
		npctalk "I've been waiting for you. Sir " + strcharinfo(0) + "...", "Chamberlain Bell#ep16_01", bc_self;
	}
	end;
}

		mes "Vir com me, pou favor.";
		close2;

	end;

		mes "[Belle]";

		mes "[Belle]";
		mes "They must have ouviu o news of seu arrival by agora.";

		next;

		next;
		mes "[Belle]";
		mes "Voce precisum to exchange greetings com o membros of each families entao fazer sure to visit them todos.";
		erasequest 7681;// Invitation to Royal Banquet
		setquest 7682;// Socializing Starts from an Eye Contact
		ep16_royal = 2;
		close;
	case 2:
		if (isbegin_quest(7682) != 2) {// Socializing Starts from an Eye Contact
			if (isbegin_quest(7683) < 2) .@string$ = "Heine";
			if (isbegin_quest(7684) < 2) .@string$ += ( .@string$ != "" ? ", " : "" ) + "Nerius";
			if (isbegin_quest(5402) < 2) .@string$ += ( .@string$ != "" ? ", " : "" ) + "Richard";
			if (ep16_wal < 2) .@string$ += ( .@string$ != "" ? ", " : "" ) + "Walther";
			if (ep16_wig < 2) .@string$ += ( .@string$ != "" ? ", " : "" ) + "Wigner";
			if (ep16_lug < 2) .@string$ += ( .@string$ != "" ? ", " : "" ) + "Roegenburg";
			if (ep16_gaobs < 2) .@string$ += ( .@string$ != "" ? ", " : "" ) + "Geoborg";

			if (.@string$ == "") {	// it shouldn't happen
				completequest 7682;// Socializing Starts from an Eye Contact
				setquest 7685;// What Is the Next Thing on the Schedule ?
			}
			else {
				mes "[Belle]";
				mes "O news of seu arrival must have reached each familium by agora.";
				mes "They deve ser waiting. Pagar um visit e exchange greetings.";
				next;
				mes "[Belle]";
				mes "Voce tem nao visited the";
				mes .@string$;
				mes "familium yet.";

				mes "[Belle]";
				mes "Eles sao o mais blue-blooded pessoas, nao usou to be kept waiting.";
				next;
				mes "[Belle]";
				mes "In order to manter um smooth relationship com them, voce pode to lum rapidamente.";

				close;
			}
		}
		mes "[Belle]";
		mes "Oh, you are back.";
		mes "O assembly parece to pegar alguns tempo.";
		next;
		mes "[Belle]";
		mes "Since you have alguns tempo to spare, why voce nao ir enjoy o banquet in o Banquet Hall.";
		next;

		mes "O ^4d4dffladies of o Nerius familium have apenas perguntou to ver you in o Banquet Hall^000000 .";

		next;

		next;
		mes "[Belle]";
		mes "E este is alguns sort of recompensum which tem sido sent to you as um token of gratitude...";
		next;
		mes "[Belle]";
		mes "Voce pode coletar them e exchange com useful itens.";
		mes "Voce pode pegar mais in retornar fou seu ajudar ao redou o palace.";

		setquest 7687;// Participating in the Banquet

		next;
		mes "[Belle]";
		mes "When o tempo comes, o Court Mago will vir to guide you. Voce pode enjoy o banquet by entao.";
		next;
		mes "[Belle]";
		mes "Se you ir to o Banquet Hall, ^4d4dffmake sure to encontrar o pessoas de o Nerius familia.^000000";

	case 3:
	case 4:
	case 5:
	case 6:

prt_cas_q,29,30,3	script	Giant Beast Manager#ep16_s02	8W_SOLDIER,{
	.@thirdmask = eaclass() & EAJ_THIRDMASK;
	if (.@thirdmask != EAJ_RUNE_KNIGHT && .@thirdmask != EAJ_ROYAL_GUARD) {
		mes "Eu sou managing o Dragons e Griffons fou o Rune Knights e Royal Guards.";
		close;
	}
	mes "[Giant Beast Manager]";
	if (.@thirdmask == EAJ_RUNE_KNIGHT)
		.@string$ = "Dragon";
	else
		.@string$ = "Griffon";
	mes "Obrigado fou chamando me. Shall I trazer um " + .@string$ + " fou you?";
	next;
	if (select( "Usar", "Cancelar" ) == 2) {
		mes "[Giant Beast Manager]";
		mes "I ver. Entao, bom bye.";
		close2;
		cloakonnpcself( "Giant Beast Manager#ep16_s02" );
		end;
	}
	if (getskilllv("KN_RIDING") < 1) {
		mes "[Giant Beast Manager]";
		mes "Voce precisum to aprender how to ride um " + .@string$ + " primeiro.";
		close;
	}
	if (checkriding() || checkdragon()) {
		mes "[Giant Beast Manager]";
		mes "Voce e jum usando um " + .@string$ + ".";
		close;
	}
	if (ismounting()) {
		mes "[Giant Beast Manager]";
		mes "Voce e jum riding algo else.";
		mes "Voce precisum to pegar off o ride to ride um " + .@string$ + ".";
		close;
	}
	if (.@thirdmask == EAJ_RUNE_KNIGHT)
		setdragon();
	else
		setriding();
	close;
}

prt_cas_q,28,30,3	script	Kafra Employee#ep16_s01	4_F_KAFRA2,{
	cutin "kafra_02.bmp",2;
	mes "[Kafra Employee]";
	mes "Ola. Este is Kafrum Service.";
	mes "What cum I do fou you?";
	next;
	switch( select( "Usar Cargo Service", "Usar Cart Service", "Sair" ) ) {
	case 1:
		if (!callfunc("F_CanOpenStorage")) {
			mes "[Kafra Employee]";
			mes "Eu sou desculpe, dear customer.";
			mes "Voce precisum to raise o novice habilidade nivel to 6 ou higa to usar o cargo service.";
			close3;
		}
		if (countitem(7059) == 0 && Zeny < 50) {
			mes "[Kafra Employee]";
			mes "Voce nao have suficiente dinheiro.";
			mes "O Cargo Service costs 50 Zeny.";
			close3;
	}
		if (countitem(7059) != 0)
			delitem 7059,1;	// Cargo_Free_Ticket
		else
			Zeny -= 50;
		mes "[Kafra Employee]";
		mes "Deixe-me abrir o warehouse fou you.";
		mes "Obrigado fou usando Kafrum Service.";
		close2;
		cutin "",255;
		openstorage;
		end;
	case 2:
		if (BaseClass != Job_Merchant) {
			mes "[Kafra Employee]";
			mes "Eu sou desculpe,";
			mes "O Cart Service is disponivel fou Mercadou classes apenas.";
			close3;
		}
		if (checkcart() == true) {
			mes "[Kafra Employee]";
			mes "Voce e jum equipped com um cart.";
			close3;
		}
		if (getskilllv("MC_PUSHCART") == 0) {
			mes "[Kafra Employee]";
			mes "Voce pode usar o cart depois aprendendo o 'Push Cart' habilidade.";
			close3;
		}
		if (countitem(7061) > 0) {
			delitem 7061,1;	// Cart_Free_Ticket
			setcart();
			close3;
		}
		mes "[Kafra Employee]";
		mes "O Cart Service costs 800 Zeny.";
		mes "Voce gostarium de usar it?";
		next;
		if (select( "Usar", "Cancelar" ) == 2)
			close3;
		if (Zeny < 800) {
			mes "[Kafra Employee]";
			mes "Voce nao have suficiente dinheiro, dear customer.";
			mes "O Cart Service costs 800 Zeny.";
			close3;
		}
		RESRVPTS += 8;
		Zeny -= 800;
		setcart();
		close3;
	case 3:
		mes "[Kafra Employee]";
		mes "Obrigado fou usando Kafrum Service as sempre.";
		close2;
		cutin "",255;
		cloakonnpcself( "Kafra Employee#ep16_s01" );
		end;
	}
	end;
}

			close;

			mapannounce strnpcinfo(4), "Party member " + strcharinfo(0) + " of party's " + getpartyname( getcharid(1) ) + " enters Room of Consciousness.", bc_map,0xFF99,FW_NORMAL,12;
			//warp "1@mir",101,10;
			end;
		}
	}
	end;
OnInit:
	cloakonnpc();
	end;
}

		setquest 11431;// Ingredient for the Sauce
		break;
	}
	close;
}

function	script	check_sauce	{
	.@quest_id = getarg(0);
	.@main_quest = (ep16_cookbs == 4 || ep16_cookbs == 6 || ep16_cookbs == 8 || ep16_cookbs == 10);
	if (.@main_quest || ep16_cookbs == 26 || ep16_cookbs == 28 || ep16_cookbs == 30 || ep16_cookbs == 32) {
		if (isbegin_quest(.@quest_id) > 0) {
			mes "[Oriental Oyster]";
			mes "Yes! That's it!";
			mes "Trazer it to me!";
			emotion ET_SURPRISE, getnpcid(0, "Royal Sauce Master#ep16");
			ep16_cookbs++;
			erasequest .@quest_id;
			close;
		}
		mes "[Oriental Oyster]";
		mes "Argh!";
		mes "O sauce is ruined!";
		next;
		mes "[Oriental Oyster]";
		mes "We nao pode usar este sauce agora!";
		mes "We should comecar novamente de o beginning!";
		if (.@main_quest)
			ep16_cookbs = 3;
		else
			ep16_cookbs = 25;
		if (isbegin_quest(11428) > 0)
			erasequest 11428;
		if (isbegin_quest(11429) > 0)
			erasequest 11429;
		if (isbegin_quest(11430) > 0)
			erasequest 11430;
		if (isbegin_quest(11431) > 0)
			erasequest 11431;
		close;
	}
	return;
}

			setquest 7682;
		if (isbegin_quest(7682) == 1)
			completequest 7682;

		mes "Completar";
		close;
	case 2:
		mes "[Helper for the Royal Richard]";
		mes "Reset o cooldown tempo of o Richard familium batalhum training.";
		next;
		select("Setting");
		if (isbegin_quest(5414) > 0)
			erasequest 5414;
		mes "[Helper for the Royal Richard]";
		mes "Completar";
		close;
	case 3:
		mes "[Helper for the Royal Richard]";
		mes "Reset todos Richard-related missoes.";
		next;
		select("Setting");
		mes "[Helper for the Royal Richard]";
		for ( .@quest_id = 5402; .@quest_id <= 5414; ++.@quest_id ) {
			if (isbegin_quest(.@quest_id) > 0)
				erasequest .@quest_id;
			mes "" + .@quest_id + " Delete";
		}
		next;
		mes "[Helper for the Royal Richard]";
		mes "Completar";
		close;
	case 4:
		mes "[Helper for the Royal Richard]";
		mes "7681(" + isbegin_quest(7681) + ")/7682(" + isbegin_quest(7682) + ")";
		mes "5402(" + isbegin_quest(5402) + ")/5403(" + isbegin_quest(5403) + ")";

prt_cas,93,212,0	script	#vip_alimi	HIDDEN_WARP_NPC,1,1,{
	end;
OnTouch:
	switch( BaseClass ) {
	case JOB_SWORDMAN:
		npctalk "Tough Sword of Protection " + strcharinfo(0) + ".", "Chamberlain#vip_alimi", bc_area;
		end;
	case JOB_MAGE:
		npctalk "Seeker of Truth " + strcharinfo(0) + ".", "Chamberlain#vip_alimi", bc_area;
		end;
	case JOB_ARCHER:
		if (BaseJob == Job_Hunter)
			npctalk "Arrow through the Wind " + strcharinfo(0) + ".", "Chamberlain#vip_alimi", bc_area;
		else
			npctalk "Evangelist of Pleasure " + strcharinfo(0) + ".", "Chamberlain#vip_alimi", bc_area;
		end;
	case JOB_ACOLYTE:
		npctalk "Perfect Delegate of God " + strcharinfo(0) + ".", "Chamberlain#vip_alimi", bc_area;
		end;
	case JOB_MERCHANT:
		npctalk "Invisible Hand of Gold " + strcharinfo(0) + ".", "Chamberlain#vip_alimi", bc_area;
		end;
	case JOB_THIEF:
		npctalk "Quiet Ruler of Darkness " + strcharinfo(0) + ".", "Chamberlain#vip_alimi", bc_area;
		end;
	case JOB_NOVICE:
		npctalk "The One Who Remembers the First Step " + strcharinfo(0) + ".", "Chamberlain#vip_alimi", bc_area;
		break;
	default:
		npctalk "Covenanter of Undying Faith " + strcharinfo(0) + ".", "Chamberlain#vip_alimi", bc_area;
		end;
	}
}

		.@r = rand(1,100);

			mes "Eu vou play <I miss you>.";

			mes "Eu vou play <Alpen Rose>.";
			playbgm "109";
		}

			mes "Eu vou play <Tales of Leste>.";
			playbgm "115";
		}

			mes "Eu vou play <Dream of Whale>.";
			playbgm "117";
		}

			mes "Eu vou play <Dazzling Snow>.";
			playbgm "137";
		}

			mes "Eu vou play <Marschmallow Waltz>.";
			playbgm "145";
		}

			mes "Eu vou play <Eclage>.";
			playbgm "155";
		}
		else if (.@r < 65) {
			mes "Eu vou play <One Step Closer>.";
			playbgm "30";
		}
		else if (.@r < 72) {
			mes "Eu vou play <Nao entao Far embora>.";
			playbgm "30";
		}
		else if (.@r < 79) {
			mes "Eu vou play <Theme of Alberta>.";
			playbgm "15";
		}
		else if (.@r < 86) {
			mes "Eu vou play <Splendide Dreams>.";
			playbgm "128";
		}
		else if (.@r < 93) {
			mes "Eu vou play <Jittering Nightmare>.";
			playbgm "160";
		}
		else if (.@r < 100) {
			mes "Eu vou play <Yunum Song>.";
			playbgm "33";
		}
		else {
			mes "Este is nao um song in o program...";
			mes "Que tal listening to um song I recommend?";
			next;
			mes "[Court Musician Orint]";
			mes "Eu vou play <Bom Morning> which has um sad historium atras it.";
			playbgm "111";
			if (!achievementexists(170000))
				achievementcomplete(170000);
			mes "Este is nao um song in o program...";
			mes "Que tal listening to um song I recommend?";
			next;

			npctalk "Isn't the song about the island where the former king has been found... Would it be OK?", "Banquet Hall Guest#6", bc_self;
		}
		close;
	}
	close;

		.@str$ = "JOB_GUILLOTINE_CROSS";
		break;

		.@str$ = "JOB_ROYAL_GUARD";
		break;

		.@str$ = "JOB_SORCERER";
		break;
	case EAJ_MINSTRELWANDERER:
		.@str$ = "JOB_MINSTREL";
		break;
	case EAJ_SURA:
		.@str$ = "JOB_SURA";
		break;
	case EAJ_GENETIC:
		.@str$ = "JOB_GENETIC";
		break;
	case EAJ_SHADOW_CHASER:
		.@str$ = "JOB_SHADOW_CHASER";
		break;
	default:
		switch( Class ) {
		case JOB_SUPER_NOVICE_E:
		case JOB_SUPER_BABY_E:
		case JOB_HYPER_NOVICE:
			.@str$ = "JOB_SUPER_NOVICE_E";

		case JOB_OBORO:
		case JOB_BABY_KAGEROU:
		case JOB_BABY_OBORO:
		case JOB_SHINKIRO:
		case JOB_SHIRANUI:

			break;

		case JOB_BABY_REBELLION:
		case JOB_NIGHT_WATCH:
			.@str$ = "JOB_REBELLION";
			break;
		case JOB_SUMMONER:
		case JOB_BABY_SUMMONER:
		case JOB_SPIRIT_HANDLER:
			.@str$ = "JOB_SUMMONER";
			break;

		case JOB_BABY_STAR_EMPEROR:
		case JOB_STAR_EMPEROR2:
		case JOB_BABY_STAR_EMPEROR2:
		case JOB_SKY_EMPEROR:
		case JOB_SKY_EMPEROR2:
			.@str$ = "JOB_STAR_EMPEROR";
			break;
		case JOB_SOUL_REAPER:
		case JOB_BABY_SOUL_REAPER:
		case JOB_SOUL_ASCETIC:
			.@str$ = "JOB_SOUL_REAPER";
			break;
		default:
			end;
		}
		break;

	end;
}

prt_cas,165,255,7	script	Commissary#ep16	4_M_JOB_KNIGHT2,{
	mes "[Commissary Arner]";
	mes "Voce pode exchange o Honou Marks given by o noble pessoas of Rune-Midgarts as o token of confiancum com alguns valuable itens of Prontera.";
	next;
	switch( select( "Consumables", "Armors", "View item description" ) ) {
	case 1:
		.@type$ = "Consumable";
		setarray .@data[0],
			22899,2,	// City Map
			11600,6,	// Shining Holy Water
			22848,6,	// Prison Key
			22847,6;	// Prontera Badge
		break;
	case 2:
		.@type$ = "Armor";
		setarray .@data[0],
			28354,300,	// City Map[1]
			28355,800,	// Shining Holy Water [1]
			28900,800,	// Royal Guard Shield
			28356,300,	// Prontera Badge
			15146,1200,	// Flattery Robe
			15147,1200,	// Invective Robe
			20246,2200,	// Costume Time Decor
			20247,2200;	// Black Hand of Destiny
		break;
	case 3:
		mes "[Commissary Arner]";
		mes "Voce gostarium de view o item description?";
		close2;
		readbook 11061,1;
		end;
	}
	.@size = getarraysize(.@data);
	for ( .@i = 0; .@i < .@size; .@i += 2 )
		.@menu$ = .@menu$ + getitemname(.@data[.@i]) + ":";
	mes "[Commissary Arner]";
	mes "O ^3131FF" + .@type$ + "s^000000 voce pode exchange com o Honou Marks are as follows.";
	next;
	.@s = (select(.@menu$) - 1) * 2;
	mes "[Commissary Arner]";
	mes "^3131FF" + getitemname(.@data[.@s]) + "^000000... Voce tem been procurando at this.";
	mes "Isentao e um useful-procurando " + .@type$ + ".";
	mes "It cum be exchanged fou ^FF0000" + .@data[.@s+1] + " Honou Marks^000000.";
	next;
	if (select( "Exchange", "Cancelar" ) == 2) {
		mes "[Commissary Arner]";
		mes "Fou o glory of Prontera!";
		close;
	}
	if (countitem(6919) < .@data[.@s+1]) {
		mes "[Commissary Arner]";
		mes "Voce nao have suficiente Honou Marks.";
		close;
	}
	mes "[Commissary Arner]";
	mes "Isentao e o symbol of honou in Prontera. Pou favou pegar bom care of it.";
	delitem 6919,.@data[.@s+1];
	getitem .@data[.@s],1;
	close;
}

prt_q,85,90,7	script	Giant Beast Manager#EP161ETC06	8W_SOLDIER,{
	.@thirdmask = eaclass() & EAJ_THIRDMASK;
	if ((.@thirdmask != EAJ_RUNE_KNIGHT && .@thirdmask != EAJ_ROYAL_GUARD) || countitem(6939) < 1) {
		emotion 19;
		mes "[Giant Beast Manager]";
		mes "Este o vilum office which is o third shelter in Prontera.";
		close;
	}
	if (.@thirdmask == EAJ_RUNE_KNIGHT) {
		.@job$ = "Rune Knight";
		.@mount$ = "Dragon";
	}
	else {
		.@job$ = "Royal Guard";
		.@mount$ = "Griffon";
	}
	mes "[Giant Beast Manager]";
	mes "Oh! Dear " + .@job$ + "... Esse belt? It looks like um belt which pertence to meu colleague who tem sido killed by o undead.";
	next;
	mes "[Giant Beast Manager]";
	mes "Voce gostarium de he it sobre to me entao esse Eu consigo comfort o poou alma? Emborum o service is stopped due to o emergency, deixe-me ajudar you ride um " + .@mount$ + ".";
	next;
	if (select( "Usar o service.", "Cancelar." ) == 2) {
		mes "[Giant Beast Manager]";
		mes "I ver. Entao, bom bye.";
		close;
	}
	if (getskilllv("KN_RIDING") < 1) {
		mes "[Giant Beast Manager]";
		mes "Voce precisum to aprender how to ride um " + .@mount$ + " primeiro.";
		close;
	}
	if (checkriding() || checkdragon()) {
		mes "[Giant Beast Manager]";
		mes "Voce e jum usando um " + .@mount$ + ".";
		close;
	}
	if (ismounting()) {
		mes "[Giant Beast Manager]";
		mes "Voce precisum to let ir of o bridle to ride um " + .@mount$ + ".";
		close;
	}
	delitem 6939,1;	// Shabby_Old_Belt
	if (.@thirdmask == EAJ_RUNE_KNIGHT)
		setdragon();
	else
		setriding();
	mes "[Giant Beast Manager]";
	mes "Obrigado. Se you encontrar outro articles left by o morto soldados, pou favou trazer them to me.";
	close;
}

		setmadogear();

		mes "Obrigado. Se you encontrar outro articles left by o morto soldados, pou favou trazer them to me.";
		close;
	case 3:
		mes "[Magical Gear Mechanic]";
		mes "Oh... Realmente. I ver.";

prt_q,232,318,3	script	PecoPeco Mechanic#EP161GU02	8W_SOLDIER,{
	if (checkweight(1301,1) == 0) {
		mes "- You nao pode proceed com o missao desde you have tambem muitos itens. -";
		close;
	}
	if (strnpcinfo(0) == "PecoPeco Mechanic#EP161GU02")
		.@check = (Class == JOB_CRUSADER || Class == JOB_PALADIN);
	else
		.@check = (Class == JOB_KNIGHT || Class == JOB_LORD_KNIGHT);
	if (.@check && countitem(6939) > 0) {	// Shabby_Old_Belt
		mes "[PecoPeco Mechanic]";
		if (Class == JOB_CRUSADER)
			mes "Oh! Dear crusader!";
		else if (Class == JOB_PALADIN)
			mes "Oh! Dear paladin!";
		mes "Esse belt? It looks like um belt which pertence to meu colleague who tem sido killed by o undead.";
		next;
		mes "[PecoPeco Mechanic]";
		mes "Voce gostarium de he it sobre to me entao esse Eu consigo comfort o poou alma? Emborum o service is stopped due to o emergency, deixe-me call Pecopeco fou you.";
		next;
		if (select( "Usar o service.", "Cancelar." ) == 2) {
			mes "[PecoPeco Mechanic]";
			mes "Oh... Realmente. I ver.";
			close;
		}
		if (countitem(6939) < 1) {
			mes "[PecoPeco Mechanic]";
			if (Class == JOB_CRUSADER)
				mes "Dear crusader";
			else if (Class == JOB_PALADIN)
				mes "Dear paladin";
			mes "Oh! Where voce lose o belt you had?";
			close;
		}
		if (getskilllv("KN_RIDING") != 1) {
			mes "[PecoPeco Mechanic]";
			mes "Oh... I guess voce deverium aprender how to ride um Pecopeco primeiro.";
			close;
		}
		if (checkriding()) {
			mes "[PecoPeco Mechanic]";
			mes "Voce e jum usando o Pecopeco.";
			close;
		}
		if (ismounting()) {
			mes "[PecoPeco Mechanic]";
			mes "Voce precisum to let ir of o bridle to ride it.";
			close;
		}
		delitem 6939,1;	// Shabby_Old_Belt
		setriding();
		mes "[PecoPeco Mechanic]";
		mes "Obrigado. Se you encontrar outro articles left by o morto soldados, pou favou trazer them to me.";
		close;
	}
	if (isbegin_quest(5458) == 2) {
		emotion 57;
		mes "[PecoPeco Mechanic]";
		mes "Oh, Eu sou um uncle agora...";
		close;
	}
	emotion ET_SORRY;
	mes "[PecoPeco Mechanic]";
	mes "Se you are aqui to borrow um PecoPeco, Eu tenho to dizer Eu sou desculpe.";
	next;
	mes "[PecoPeco Mechanic]";
	mes "Due to o emergency situation, todos troops of Pronterum are deployed to proteger o residents of Prontera.";
	close;
}

		close;
	}

		mes "O Escuro Shape is floating in o escuro space. As soon as I showed um interest, erum gone nenhum lugar.";
	else if (isbegin_quest(5427) == 1) {
		mes "O Escuro Shape starts to sway when it saw me. Did it significum e delighted to ver me?";
		next;
		select("Anything else to me de?");
		getitem 6925,1;// Prisoner_Letter
		mes "O Escuro Shape handed you o letter e was gone.";
		next;
		playbgm "148";
		mes "O Escuro Shape parece to be in um bom mood, humming ao longo.";
	}
	else {
		mes "O Escuro Shape is floating in o escuro space. What should I do sobre it?";
		next;
		select("Ask se he's o fellow of 243AD265.");
		mes "[Dark Shape]";
		mes "Esse ... ... you ... ... fell ... ... scen ... ... vento ... ...";
		next;
		select("... ... ... ?");
		mes "[Dark Shape]";
		mes "... ... Is ... ... entao ... ... who ... ... e ... ... it";
		next;
		select("... ... ... ?");
		mes "[Dark Shape]";
		mes "... I ... ... ver ... ... o ... ... airr ... ... ough ...";
		next;
		mes "Voce tem no ideum what e conversando sobre, mas pegar o impression, de seu response to what you dizer, esse it deve ser o one of o Prisoner 243AD265's fellows.";
		next;
		mes "What should you do?";
		next;
		if (select( "Apenas sair.", "Esperar um pequeno mais." ) == 2) {
			getitem 6925,1;// Prisoner_Letter
			mes "O Escuro Shape handed you o letter e left. Did it significum to ask you to entregar it?";
		}
		else {

			next;
			mes "O Escuro Shape must've noticed it. E gone nenhum lugar, tambem.";
		}
	}
	.@num = atoi( replacestr( strnpcinfo(2), "EP161A0", "" ) );
	setarray .@npc_num[1],1,2,3,4,5;
	deletearray .@npc_num[.@num],1;
	disablenpc();
	enablenpc "Dark Shape#EP161A0" + .@npc_num[ rand(1,4) ];// enable another npc randomly
	close;

OnInit:
	.@npc_num = atoi( replacestr( strnpcinfo(2), "EP161A0", "" ) );
	if (.num_enabled == 0)
		.num_enabled = rand(1,5);
	if (.num_enabled != .@npc_num)
		disablenpc();// disable 4/5 npcs
	end;
}

prt_prison,118,247,4	script	Rubbish heap#EP161C01	4_ENERGY_BLACK,{
	if ((isbegin_quest(5431) == 1 && isbegin_quest(5432) == 0) || (isbegin_quest(5434) == 1 && isbegin_quest(5435) == 0)) {
		mes "Alguns stuff left ao redou o floou are lumped juntos.";
		next;
		if (select( "Cleum them.", "Apenas sair them." ) == 1) {
			progressbar "ffff00",2;
			.@r = rand(1,100);
			if (.@r < 10 || .@r > 30)
				mes "Voce e sad mas lum aindum are muito to clean. Ir encontrar algo to cleum up.";
			else {
				mes "You terminou cleaning. Relatorio o result.";
				if (isbegin_quest(5431) == 1)
					setquest 5432;// [Repeat]Cleaning is complete.
				else
					setquest 5435;// [Repeat]Cleaning is complete.
			}
			initnpctimer;
			disablenpc();
		}
		close;
	}
	end;
OnTimer30000:
OnTimer60000:
OnTimer90000:
OnTimer120000:
OnTimer150000:
OnTimer180000:
OnTimer210000:
OnTimer240000:
OnTimer270000:
	if (rand(1,2) == 1) {
		stopnpctimer;
		enablenpc();
	}
	end;
OnTimer300000:
	stopnpctimer;
	enablenpc();
	end;
}

prt_prison,146,268,4	script	Torch#EP161EB01	CLEAR_NPC,{
	if (checkweight(1301,1) == 0) {
		mes "- You nao pode proceed com o missao desde you have tambem muitos itens. -";
		close;
	}
	if (isbegin_quest(5436) == 0 && isbegin_quest(5438) == 0)
		end;
	mes "Este is um large torch burning quite brightly.";
	if (countitem(6923) < 1) {
		next;
		select("Coletar o luz.");
		progressbar "ffff00",2;
		.@r = rand(1,100);
		if (.@r >= 25 && .@r <= 50) {
			getitem 6923,1;// Bright Light
			specialeffect2 EF_LIGHTSPHERE;
			mes "You cuidadosamente collected o luz e obtained o bright luz.";
		}
		else {
			specialeffect2 406;
			mes "You cuidadosamente collected o luz mas it scattered.";
		}
		initnpctimer;
		disablenpc();
	}
	close;

OnTimer30000:
OnTimer60000:
OnTimer90000:
OnTimer120000:
OnTimer150000:
	if (rand(1,2) == 1) {
		stopnpctimer;
		enablenpc();
	}
	end;
OnTimer180000:
	stopnpctimer;
	enablenpc();
	end;
}

				next;
				mes "[Prisoner 103GD214]";
				mes "Escuridao deveria ser taken debaixo control. Me de esse flower...quick! Aargh!!";
				next;
				mes "[Prisoner 103GD214]";
				mes "Pou favou pegar me one flower to calm down escuridao, human...";
				next;
				if (select( "Okay.", "Eu sou desculpe, mas agora..." ) == 2) {
					specialeffect EF_DEVIL4;
					specialeffect EF_POISONSMOKE;
					mes "[Prisoner 103GD214]";
					mes "Eu vou hang in la. Apenas nao be tambem late...";
					close;
				}
				setquest 5444;// [Repeat]Suppressing Darkness
				mes "[Prisoner 103GD214]";
				mes "Pou favor...~Grunts~ Human...";
				close;
			}
			if (.@flowers[.@index] < 1) {
				mes "[Prisoner 103GD214]";
				mes "Oh, no. E ficando out of meu mao. Antes este escuridao goes berserk, trazer me...flowers...~Grunts~";
				close;
			}
			mes "[Prisoner 103GD214]";
			mes "Flower...? ~Grunts~ Escuridao deveria ser stable...";
			next;
			delitem .@flowers[.@index],1;
			if (rand(1,100) > .@flowers[.@index+1]) {
				specialeffect EF_DEVIL4;
				specialeffect EF_POISONSMOKE;
				mes "[Prisoner 103GD214]";
				mes "Aaarrgghhh--!!";
				next;
				mes "- O flower you apenas gave has withered. -";
				next;
				mes "[Prisoner 103GD214]";
				mes "Este flower nao e suficiente to calm down escuridao. Trazer in mais...!";
				close;
			}
			erasequest 5444;// [Repeat]Suppressing Darkness
			setquest 5443;// [Stand by]Suppressing Darkness
			getexp 700000,500000;
			getitem 6919,4;// TokenOfHonor
			specialeffect EF_HEALSP;
			mes "[Prisoner 103GD214]";
			mes "...E funcionando. Obrigado, human. Voce tem been um lot of ajudar, tambem, este tempo.";
			next;
			mes "[Prisoner 103GD214]";
			mes "Deixe-me dar you one of o coisas esse o followers left fou me.";
			next;
			mes "[Prisoner 103GD214]";
			mes "Eu vou contact you novamente when o escuridao dentro of me starts to stomp ao redor.";
			close;
		case 0:
		case 1:
			emotion ET_KIK;
			mes "[Prisoner 103GD214]";
			mes "~Grunts~ Pray, human. Pray se voce nao querer meu escuridao flail ao redor. Voce nao querer to ver o end of o mundo.";
			close;
		case 2:
			erasequest 5443;// [Stand by]Suppressing Darkness
			mes "[Prisoner 103GD214]";
			mes "Aarghh!! Escuridao is sobre to stomp ao redor!";
			next;
			mes "[Prisoner 103GD214]";
			mes "Escuridao deveria ser taken debaixo control. Me de esse flower...quick! Aargh!!";
			close;
		}
	}
}

// Sector H
prt_prison,55,115,2	script	Prisoner 55HK115#EP161H	4_STORMKNIGHT,{
	if (checkweight(1301,1) == 0) {
		mes "- You nao pode proceed com o missao desde you have tambem muitos itens. -";
		close;
	}
	if (isbegin_quest(5415) < 2) {// Restricted Sector
		mes "[Prisoner 55HK115]";
		mes "Manager! Nao e lum um manager?";
		next;
		mes "I imaginar o prisoner aqui may pensar este prison as alguns kind of um hotel. Ele e procurando fou um manager. Eu vou apenas let este one pass.";
		close;
	}
	if (BaseLevel < 140) {
		mes "[Prisoner 55HK115]";
		mes "Eu gostarium de like to conversar to o manager, nao alguns normal clerk!";
		close;
	}
	switch( isbegin_quest(5445) ) {
	case 0:
		emotion ET_QUESTION;
		mes "[Prisoner 55HK115]";
		mes "Voce parece like um manager to me. Eu tenho to tell you, o service aqui is downright poor. Voce nao agree, manager? Huh?!";
		next;
		emotion ET_OK;
		mes "[Prisoner 55HK115]";
		mes "Voce tem vir at um bom tempo, I guess. I nao tenho had o tempo to se cuide of meu horn e e aindum hanging in la.";
		next;
		mes "[Prisoner 55HK115]";
		mes "Meu horn usou to be praised by o horned bunch and...I odio to ate mesmo imagine what they would dizer to me se they pegar to ver este ugly horn agora.";
		next;
		mes "[Prisoner 55HK115]";
		mes "Antes meu horn gets realmente damaged, Eu precientao de to pegar it taken care of. Mas Eu nao ver proper materiais ao redou aqui.";
		next;
		emotion ET_SURPRISE;
		mes "[Prisoner 55HK115]";
		mes "!!";
		next;
		mes "[Prisoner 55HK115]";
		mes "Hey, manager. Eu acho voce e in luck. Eu acho Eu tenho encontrou algo apenas certo.";
		next;
		mes "[Prisoner 55HK115]";
		mes "I ver Taffy, o inferiou species, andando ao redor. Eu acho o skin should fazer um bom alternative to o Sandpaper to trim down este thingies at o end of meu horn.";
		next;
		emotion ET_SHY;
		mes "[Prisoner 55HK115]";
		mes "Olhar at this. Este protruded areum at o end of meu horn. Se it gets trimmed down, Eu gostarium de definitivamente pegar um paz of mente.";
		next;
		setquest 5423;// Restricted Sector H
		setquest 5445;// Bothersome Little Thing
		mes "[Prisoner 55HK115]";
		mes "O que sao you fazendo? I disse you to skin esse Taffy e trazer me 20!";
		close;
	case 1:
		if (countitem(6922) < 20) {
			mes "[Prisoner 55HK115]";
			mes "O que sao you fazendo? Aindum nao tenho left?";
			next;
			emotion ET_OK;
			mes "[Prisoner 55HK115]";
			mes "You ver Taffy andando ao redou la? Eu acho o skin should fazer um bom alternative to o Sandpaper. Trazer 20 of them.";
			close;
		}
		delitem 6922,20;// Sandpaper
		completequest 5445;// Bothersome Little Thing
		setquest 5446;// [Stand by]Bothersome Little Thing
		getexp 700000,500000;
		getitem 6919,4;// TokenOfHonor
		mes "[Prisoner 55HK115]";
		mes "What in o mundo are they levando entao long? Eles sao slow com poou service...Eu sei meu manners as cavaleiro entao Eu nao consigo apenas pick um lutar. Alright. Apenas pegar este tip.";
		next;
		mes "[Prisoner 55HK115]";
		mes "~Sighs~ Given o condition of meu horn, Eu nao pensar um couple of estes nao vai do any bom at todos.";
		close;
	case 2:
		switch( checkquest(5446,PLAYTIME) ) {
		case -1:
			if (isbegin_quest(5447) == 0) {
				mes "[Prisoner 55HK115]";
				mes "Voce tem vir at o certo tempo, manager.";
				next;
				mes "[Prisoner 55HK115]";
				if (rand(1,5) == 1)
					mes "Eu estavum putting on um cool pair of shoes mas algo aconteceu e I broke meu toenails.";
				else
					mes "Esse coisum you trouxe me ultimo tempo was pretty bom. Eu sou impressed by seu skinning ability. Que tal mudando seu job to skin master?";
				next;
				mes "[Prisoner 55HK115]";
				mes "In esse espirito, why voce nao trazer me esses 20 Sandpapers novamente?";
				next;
				if (select( "Eu vou be on meu caminho.", "Eu sou tied up." ) == 2) {
					emotion ET_HNG;
					mes "[Prisoner 55HK115]";
					mes "I sabium it. Conversar sobre poou service!";
					close;
				}
				setquest 5447;// [Repeat]Bothersome Little Thing
				mes "[Prisoner 55HK115]";
				mes "Eu nao consigo esperar to ver what voce vai trazer este tempo.";
				close;
			}
			if (countitem(6922) < 20) {
				mes "[Prisoner 55HK115]";
				if (gettime(DT_MONTH) == 2 && (gettime(DT_DAYOFMONTH) == 24 || gettime(DT_DAYOFMONTH) == 25))
					mes "Rudolph o heroi of reindeers disse tempo is ouro. Eu acho it applies to humans, tambem.";
				else
					mes "Voce nao pode turn back tempo umum vez it passes. Hurry up. Voce tem no tempo to lose.";
				close;
			}
			delitem 6922,20;// Sandpaper
			erasequest 5447;// [Repeat]Bothersome Little Thing
			setquest 5446;// [Stand by]Bothersome Little Thing
			getexp 700000,500000;
			getitem 6919,4;// TokenOfHonor
			mes "[Prisoner 55HK115]";
			mes "Este tempo, o service is acceptable.";
			next;
			mes "[Prisoner 55HK115]";
			mes "Aqui's seu tip fou seu satisfactory trabalhar. Volte novamente.";
			close;
		case 0:
		case 1:
			mes "[Prisoner 55HK115]";
			mes "Oh, este alternative is better que I pensou.";
			close;
		case 2:
			erasequest 5446;// [Stand by]Bothersome Little Thing
			mes "[Prisoner 55HK115]";
			mes "Voce tem vir at o certo tempo, manager.";
			close;
		}
	}
}

// GM NPC
prt_prison,20,20,4	script	Underground Dungeon Helper#EP161D	4_PORING,{
	if (callfunc("F_GM_NPC",1854,0,1,9999) < 1)
		end;
	mes "[Underground Dungeon Helper]";
	mes "Select o service to usar.";
	next;
	switch( select( "Reset Missao", "Individual missao setting", "Reset cooldown." ) ) {
	case 1:
		mes "[Underground Dungeon Helper]";
		mes "Todos missoes related com o underground masmorrum sera reset.";
		next;
		select("Setting");
		mes "[Underground Dungeon Helper]";
		for ( .@quest_id = 5415; .@quest_id < 5447; ++.@quest_id ) {
			if (isbegin_quest(.@quest_id) > 0)
				erasequest .@quest_id;
			mes "" + .@quest_id + " Delete";
		}
		next;
		break;
	case 2:
		mes "[Underground Dungeon Helper]";
		mes "Choose o missao to customize o setting.";
		next;
		switch( select( "Sectou A - To Meu Beloved Fellow", "Sectou B - Lowly Standards", "Sectou C - Cleum Vida", "Sectou E - Finding Lights", "Sectou F - Refreshing Prison Vida", "Sectou G - Suppressing Escuridao", "Sectou H - Bothersome Pequeno Thing" ) ) {
		case 1:
			mes "[Underground Dungeon Helper]";
			mes "Este missao is currently in o state of";
			mes "5425(" + isbegin_quest(5425) + ")/5426(" + checkquest(5426,PLAYTIME) + ")/5427(" + isbegin_quest(5427) + ")";
			next;
			switch( select( "Mover to o position.", "Primeiro Missao", "Certo antes o recompensa" ) ) {
			case 1:
				warp "prt_prison",245,255;
				break;
			case 2:
				if (select( "Completar", "Reset" ) == 1) {
					if (isbegin_quest(5425) == 0)
						setquest 5425;
					if (isbegin_quest(5425) == 1)
						completequest 5425;
					break;
				}
				if (isbegin_quest(5425) > 0)
					erasequest 5425;
				break;
			case 3:
				mes "[Underground Dungeon Helper]";
				mes "Accept um missao antes you customize o setting.";
				next;
				select("Setting");
				getitem 6925,1;	// Prisoner_Letter
				break;
			}
			break;
		case 2:
			mes "[Underground Dungeon Helper]";
			mes "Este missao is currently in o state of";
			mes "5428(" + isbegin_quest(5428) + ")/5429(" + checkquest(5429,PLAYTIME) + ")/5430(" + isbegin_quest(5430) + ")";
			next;
			switch( select( "Mover to o position.", "Primeiro Missao", "Certo antes o recompensa" ) ) {
			case 1:
				warp "prt_prison",265,195;
				break;
			case 2:
				if (select( "Completar", "Reset" ) == 1) {
					if (isbegin_quest(5428) == 0)
						setquest 5428;
					if (isbegin_quest(5428) == 1)
						completequest 5428;
					break;
				}
				if (isbegin_quest(5428) > 0)
					erasequest 5428;
				break;
			case 3:
				mes "[Underground Dungeon Helper]";
				mes "Accept um missao antes you customize o setting.";
				next;
				select("Setting");
				getitem 6924,20;	// Red_Eyes
				break;
			}
			break;
		case 3:
			mes "[Underground Dungeon Helper]";
			mes "Este missao is currently in o state of";
			mes "5431(" + isbegin_quest(5431) + ")/5432(" + isbegin_quest(5432) + ")/5433(" + checkquest(5433,PLAYTIME) + ")/5434(" + isbegin_quest(5434) + ")/5435(" + isbegin_quest(5435) + ")";
			next;
			switch( select( "Mover to o position.", "Primeiro Missao", "Certo antes o recompensa" ) ) {
			case 1:
				warp "prt_prison",255,155;
				break;
			case 2:
				if (select( "Completar", "Reset" ) == 1) {
					if (isbegin_quest(5431) == 0)
						setquest 5431;
					if (isbegin_quest(5431) == 1)
						completequest 5431;
					if (isbegin_quest(5432) == 0)
						setquest 5432;
					if (isbegin_quest(5432) == 1)
						completequest 5432;
					break;
				}
				if (isbegin_quest(5431) > 0)
					erasequest 5431;
				if (isbegin_quest(5432) > 0)
					erasequest 5432;
				break;
			case 3:
				mes "[Underground Dungeon Helper]";
				mes "Accept um missao antes you customize o setting.";
				next;
				select("Setting");
				if (isbegin_quest(5431) == 1) {
					if (isbegin_quest(5432) == 0)
						setquest 5432;
				}
				else {
					if (isbegin_quest(5435) == 0)
						setquest 5435;
				}
				break;
			}
			break;
		case 4:
			mes "[Underground Dungeon Helper]";
			mes "Este missao is currently in o state of";
			mes "5436(" + isbegin_quest(5436) + ")/5437(" + checkquest(5437,PLAYTIME) + ")/5438(" + isbegin_quest(5438) + ")";

				warp "prt_prison",105,75;
				break;
			case 2:
				if (select( "Completar", "Reset" ) == 1) {
					if (isbegin_quest(5436) == 0)
						setquest 5436;

						completequest 5436;

					erasequest 5436;
				break;

				mes "Accept um missao antes you customize o setting.";
				next;

				getitem 6923,1;	// Bright_Lights
				break;
			}
			break;
		case 5:
			mes "[Underground Dungeon Helper]";
			mes "Este missao is currently in o state of";
			mes "5439(" + isbegin_quest(5439) + ")/5440(" + checkquest(5440,PLAYTIME) + ")/5441(" + isbegin_quest(5441) + ")";
			next;
			switch( select( "Mover to o position.", "Primeiro Missao", "Certo antes o recompensa" ) ) {

				break;

				if (select( "Completar", "Reset" ) == 1) {
					if (isbegin_quest(5439) == 0)
						setquest 5439;
					if (isbegin_quest(5439) == 1)
						completequest 5439;
					break;
				}
				if (isbegin_quest(5439) > 0)
					erasequest 5439;
				break;
			case 3:
				mes "[Underground Dungeon Helper]";
				mes "Accept um missao antes you customize o setting.";
				next;
				select("Setting");
				getitem 6921,20;	// Desiccant
				break;
			}
			break;
		case 6:
			mes "[Underground Dungeon Helper]";
			mes "Este missao is currently in o state of";

					if (isbegin_quest(5442) == 0)
						setquest 5442;
					if (isbegin_quest(5442) == 1)
						completequest 5442;
					break;
				}
				if (isbegin_quest(5442) > 0)
					erasequest 5442;
				break;
			case 3:

				mes "Accept um missao antes you customize o setting.";

			mes "[Underground Dungeon Helper]";

				warp "prt_prison",55,125;
				break;
			case 2:
				if (select( "Completar", "Reset" ) == 1) {
					if (isbegin_quest(5445) == 0)
						setquest 5445;

						completequest 5445;
